<a id="llm-methods"></a>

## LLM methods


<a id="inference-time-scaling"></a>

### Inference-time scaling

- **[Self-Consistency](#/papers/wang2022selfconsistency "Self-Consistency Improves Chain of Thought Reasoning in Language Models (2023)")**: Self-Consistency Improves Chain of Thought Reasoning in Language Models (ICLR 2023). Sample several reasoning paths and take the majority answer. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2022selfconsistency) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2203.11171) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.11171)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Large Language Monkeys](#/papers/brown2024monkeys "Large Language Monkeys: Scaling Inference Compute with Repeated Sampling (2024)")**: Scaling Inference Compute with Repeated Sampling (preprint 2024). Coverage (any sample correct) often grows log-linearly with the number of samples and can be modelled as an exponentiated power law (abstract; §1, §3.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/brown2024monkeys) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2407.21787) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21787) · [Code](https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/large_language_monkeys)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[compute-optimal test-time scaling](#/papers/snell2024scaling "Scaling LLM Test-Time Compute Optimally can be More Effective than Scaling Model Parameters (2024)")**: Scaling LLM Test-Time Compute Optimally can be More Effective than Scaling Model Parameters (preprint 2024). Compares search against verifiers with sequential revisions, by problem difficulty. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/snell2024scaling) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2408.03314) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.03314)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[s1](#/papers/muennighoff2025simple "s1: Simple test-time scaling (2025)")**: Simple test-time scaling (EMNLP 2025). Budget forcing: end or extend the model's thinking to control compute. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/muennighoff2025simple) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2501.19393) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19393) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.emnlp-main.1025) · [Code](https://github.com/simplescaling/s1)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Tree of Thoughts](#/papers/yao2023tot "Tree of Thoughts: Deliberate Problem Solving with Large Language Models (2023)")**: Deliberate Problem Solving with Large Language Models (NeurIPS 2023). Search over intermediate thoughts with self-evaluation, BFS or DFS. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/yao2023tot) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.10601) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.10601) · [Code](https://github.com/princeton-nlp/tree-of-thought-llm)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[AlphaEvolve](#/papers/novikov2025alphaevolve "AlphaEvolve: A coding agent for scientific and algorithmic discovery (2025)")**: A coding agent for scientific and algorithmic discovery (preprint 2025 (Google DeepMind white paper)). An evolutionary coding agent: LLMs propose program changes, automated evaluators score them. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/novikov2025alphaevolve) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2506.13131) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13131) · [Code](https://github.com/google-deepmind/alphaevolve_results)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[False Positives under Inference Scaling](#/papers/wang2025falsepositives "Examining False Positives under Inference Scaling for Mathematical Reasoning (2025)")**: Examining False Positives under Inference Scaling for Mathematical Reasoning (preprint 2025). Measures correct final answers reached through flawed reasoning in math, across models and datasets (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2025falsepositives) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.06217) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06217) · [Code](https://github.com/Wloner0809/False-Positives-in-Math)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/labels">labels</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[The Limits of Inference Scaling Through Resampling](#/papers/stroebl2024resampling "The Limits of Inference Scaling Through Resampling (2024)")** ((v1–v2: "Inference Scaling fLaws"), ICLR 2026 (per its code README), 2024). Argues that resampling until a verifier passes is bounded when the verifier has false positives, since resampling can't lower their probability (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/stroebl2024resampling) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2411.17501) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17501) · [Code](https://github.com/benediktstroebl/inference-scaling-limits)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Sample More, Reflect Less](#/papers/mirzaei2026samplemore "Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B (2026)")**: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B (preprint 2026). Compares seven reasoning methods (single-sample, self-inspection, best-of-N self-verification, debate) with repeated sampling at equal measured token cost, on 1.5B–7B open models (four of the seven at 7B) and two math benchmarks (abstract; methods in §4.3 Tab. 1; 7B rows in Tab. 2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/mirzaei2026samplemore) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.28576) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](#/papers/chen2026cofailure "When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models (2026)")** (preprint 2026). Any policy whose output is one member model's answer (a router, a vote, a cascade) has accuracy at most 1 − β, where β is the rate at which every model in the pool is wrong on the same query. The average pairwise error correlation ρ, the diagnostic practice reports, cannot identify β (abstract; §5, Props. 1 and 3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chen2026cofailure) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.27288) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/stats">stats</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[AlphaCode](#/papers/li2022alphacode "Competition-Level Code Generation with AlphaCode (2022)")**: Competition-Level Code Generation with AlphaCode (Science 2022). Competitive programming: an encoder-decoder transformer, pre-trained on GitHub code and fine-tuned on the authors' CodeContests dataset, samples up to millions of C++ and Python programs per problem; programs that fail the problem statement's example tests are dropped, and the rest are clustered by their outputs on inputs written by a separate test-input model, choosing at most 10 submissions (abstract; §4.4–4.6). Evaluated on CodeContests and in simulated Codeforces contests (§5.1–5.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2022alphacode) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2203.07814) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.07814) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.abq1158) · [Code](https://github.com/google-deepmind/code_contests)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag" href="#/tags/labels">labels</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-general">cex-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[AdverMCTS](#/papers/li2026advermcts "AdverMCTS: Combating Pseudo-Correctness in Code Generation via Adversarial Monte Carlo Tree Search (2026)")**: Combating Pseudo-Correctness in Code Generation via Adversarial Monte Carlo Tree Search (preprint 2026). Code generation as a game between two MCTS agents on one LLM: a Solver searches over reasoning steps and writes programs, and an Attacker searches for test inputs on which the programs that pass the public tests disagree; an LLM arbiter labels each such input's expected output, and the labelled tests then screen new programs, penalize failing branches and re-rank the final candidates (abstract; §3.3–3.6). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2026advermcts) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.10449) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10449)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-general">cex-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Selection Without Signal, Recovery Through Expression: A Measurement Study of Post-Hoc Falsification Operators for Frozen Small Code Models](#/papers/iscan2026falsification "Selection Without Signal, Recovery Through Expression: A Measurement Study of Post-Hoc Falsification Operators for Frozen Small Code Models (2026)")** (preprint 2026). Measures whether post-hoc operators over a frozen small code model's own samples (selection, verification, repair, elimination, portfolios, sound vetoes, generation conditioning) beat Best-of-N, which deploys the first candidate that passes the visible tests; correctness is the hidden tests' verdict (abstract; §2). Models of at most 1.7B run locally (Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B and 0.5B, DeepSeek-Coder 1.3B, Qwen3 probes) on a self-authored benchmark, an engineered one, HumanEval+ and MBPP+ (§3; Tab. 2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/iscan2026falsification) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.16999) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16999)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/stats">stats</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Verification Trap](#/papers/he2026verificationtrap "Verification Trap: Understanding Test-Time Selection Failures under False Premises in Code Generation (2026)")**: Understanding Test-Time Selection Failures under False Premises in Code Generation (EMNLP 2026). Inserts a false premise into coding task descriptions, with hidden tests unchanged, samples 64 candidate programs, and selects one by default with unit tests a verifier writes from the same prompt; a task is trapped when the pool held a hidden-test-correct program but the selector returned a wrong one (Trap@64) (abstract; §3.1; §4.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/he2026verificationtrap) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.05170) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05170)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[CodeT](#/papers/chen2022codet "CodeT: Code Generation with Generated Tests (2023)")**: Code Generation with Generated Tests (ICLR 2023). The same code model that samples candidate programs is prompted to write test cases (input and expected output) for the problem; every program runs on every test, programs that pass the same tests form a consensus set, and the set scored by its numbers of programs and tests supplies the answer ("dual execution agreement", RANSAC-inspired) (abstract; §2.1–2.2). Zero-shot on HumanEval, MBPP, APPS and CodeContests with three Codex models, InCoder-6B and CodeGen-16B (§3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chen2022codet) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2207.10397) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.10397) · [Code](https://github.com/microsoft/CodeT)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-general">cex-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>

- **[ReAct](#/papers/yao2022react "ReAct: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models (2023)")**: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models (ICLR 2023). A paired audit of an added model call: success before and after on the same instances, under one binary rule (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/reitich2026correction) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.18245) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18245)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Flat Score, Amplified Failures](#/papers/jang2026flatscore "Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents (2026)")**: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents (preprint 2026). Multi-turn tool-calling agents on τ²-bench at 16-, 8- and 4-bit weights (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/jang2026flatscore) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.27275) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[Solving a Million-Step LLM Task with Zero Errors](#/papers/meyerson2025maker "Solving a Million-Step LLM Task with Zero Errors (2025)")** (preprint 2025). Solves a Towers of Hanoi instance whose solution takes over one million LLM steps, with zero errors (abstract; §4.4); the solution strategy is given in every agent's prompt, so only execution is tested (§4.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/meyerson2025maker) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2511.09030) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09030) · [Code](https://github.com/cognizant-ai-lab/neuro-san-benchmarking)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[interwhen](#/papers/bhat2026interwhen "interwhen: A Generalizable Framework for Steering Reasoning Models with Test-time Verification (2026)")**: A Generalizable Framework for Steering Reasoning Models with Test-time Verification (preprint 2026). Steers a single reasoning or agent trajectory with verifiers that run alongside generation: a forked copy of the model (on τ²-bench and VitaBench, a separate Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, §5) periodically extracts verifiable states from the partial trace, and a violation goes back to the model as feedback (§1; §4.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/bhat2026interwhen) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2602.11202) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11202) · [Code](https://github.com/microsoft/interwhen)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-general">itp-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[FIRE: Failure-Informed Runtime Engineering for Reliable Language-Model Agents](#/papers/agarwal2026fire "FIRE: Failure-Informed Runtime Engineering for Reliable Language-Model Agents (2026)")** (preprint 2026). Runtime policies for a terminal coding agent (the Codex CLI on Terminal-Bench 2.1): short natural-language instructions or action denials that the harness applies when a trajectory reaches a state that preceded observed failures, with model weights and the user prompt unchanged (abstract; §3; §4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/agarwal2026fire) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.26048) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26048)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[APIFlow-Bench](#/papers/wan2026apiflowbench "APIFlow-Bench: Measuring Whether Agents Survive Long, Dependent API Workflows (2026)")**: Measuring Whether Agents Survive Long, Dependent API Workflows (preprint 2026). A benchmark of long, dependent REST-API workflows in procedurally generated mock worlds, graded deterministically on both the backend state (a mock-minted canary traced through the call path) and a typed answer card (abstract; §3; §5). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wan2026apiflowbench) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29128) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29128) · [Code](https://github.com/postmanlabs/APIFlow-Bench)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag" href="#/tags/labels">labels</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[StaminaBench](#/papers/sobal2026staminabench "StaminaBench: Stress-Testing Coding Agents over 100 Interaction Turns (2026)")**: Stress-Testing Coding Agents over 100 Interaction Turns (preprint 2026). A benchmark of how many consecutive change requests a coding agent survives: the agent implements a REST API server and then modifies it through procedurally generated schema changes, up to 100 turns (abstract; §3.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/sobal2026staminabench) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.19613) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19613) · [Code](https://github.com/amazon-science/StaminaBench)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/labels">labels</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[Attention Tax, Handoff Tax](#/papers/anchan2026handofftax "Attention Tax, Handoff Tax: A Stylised Model of When Multi-Agent LLM Systems Help (2026)")**: A Stylised Model of When Multi-Agent LLM Systems Help (preprint 2026). A stylised reliability model of single- against multi-agent LLM systems: per-step failure rises with the context an agent attends over, handing work to a fresh agent costs a "handoff tax" per boundary, and parallel samples share part of their failures (abstract; §3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/anchan2026handofftax) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.06069) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06069) · [Code](https://github.com/akshitanchan/attention-handoff-tax)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[The Hallucination Snowball](#/papers/singh2026snowball "The Hallucination Snowball: Modeling Error Propagation as State Transitions in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines (2026)")**: Modeling Error Propagation as State Transitions in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines (ICML 2026 FAGEN workshop). Injects wrong numbers into the first agent's output of a four-agent LangGraph pipeline (Researcher → Analyst → Writer → Reviewer) on FinanceBench questions and tracks at each stage whether a gpt-4o judge without ground truth and a numeric matcher against reference values still detect them (§3.1–3.2); a second experiment compares five LLMs as skeptics at the first stage (§3.3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/singh2026snowball) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.14588) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14588) · [Code](https://github.com/prabhjotschugh/hallucination-snowball)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[τ-bench](#/papers/yao2024taubench "$\tau$-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains (2025)")**: $\tau$-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains (ICLR 2025). A benchmark of customer-service agents (retail and airline domains) that use database API tools, follow a written domain policy and talk to an LLM-simulated user; each task's hidden user instruction is written so that only one final database state is possible (abstract; §3; §4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/yao2024taubench) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2406.12045) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12045) · [Code](https://github.com/sierra-research/tau-bench)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[Large Language Models Cannot Self-Correct Reasoning Yet](#/papers/huang2023selfcorrect "Large Language Models Cannot Self-Correct Reasoning Yet (2024)")** (ICLR 2024). Tests intrinsic self-correction, where a model reviews its own answer and answers again with no external feedback, on GSM8K, CommonSenseQA and HotpotQA with GPT-3.5, GPT-4, GPT-4-Turbo and Llama-2 (§2; §3.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/huang2023selfcorrect) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.01798) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.01798)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[LLM-Modulo ("LLMs Can't Plan](#/papers/kambhampati2024llmmodulo "Position: LLMs Can't Plan, But Can Help Planning in LLM-Modulo Frameworks (2024)")**: Position: LLMs Can't Plan, But Can Help Planning in LLM-Modulo Frameworks (But Can Help Planning in LLM-Modulo Frameworks"), ICML 2024 position paper). Argues that autoregressive LLMs cannot plan or verify plans by themselves, so cannot improve by critiquing their own output, drawing mainly on the authors' earlier studies (abstract; §2.1–2.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/kambhampati2024llmmodulo) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2402.01817) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01817)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[Feedback Loops With Language Models Drive In-Context Reward Hacking](#/papers/pan2024feedbackloops "Feedback Loops With Language Models Drive In-Context Reward Hacking (2024)")** (ICML 2024). When an LLM's outputs change the world it later reads, the feedback loop optimizes a proxy objective at test time and can raise harmful side effects with it, which the authors call in-context reward hacking (ICRH) (abstract; §1). Two mechanisms: output-refinement, where the LLM improves its own earlier outputs, and policy-refinement, where it changes how it acts after feedback such as API errors (§3.2, §4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/pan2024feedbackloops) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2402.06627) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06627)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a></span>
- **[Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](#/papers/asadolahi2026memoryinflation "Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents (2026)")** ((Echo Gap, LUCID), preprint 2026). A memory agent stores each episode with a score it gives itself and reuses high-scored ones; the authors call the inflated scores of wrong episodes the "Echo Gap", argue that reuse compounds it, and state an Error-Independence Assumption (EIA): a correcting signal must track truth and have errors uncorrelated with the self-grade's (abstract; §1; §3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/asadolahi2026memoryinflation) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00017) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-gen">llm-gen</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-misc">nl2sql-misc</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents](#/papers/suresh2026grounding "Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents (2026)")** (preprint 2026). After each task a separate curator agent writes the agent's long-term memory from the trajectory and its grade (§3.2); this paper gives that curator read-only environment tools to check, narrow or refresh a candidate memory before committing it ("propose–probe–commit", §3.3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/suresh2026grounding) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.11060) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11060)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[ExpeL](#/papers/zhao2023expel "ExpeL: LLM Agents Are Experiential Learners (2024)")**: LLM Agents Are Experiential Learners (AAAI 2024). An agent gathers experience on training tasks by retrying them with Reflexion, then at test time adds natural-language insights drawn from that experience, and retrieved successful trajectories, to its ReAct prompt, without weight updates (§1, §4.1–§4.3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhao2023expel) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2308.10144) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.10144) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i17.29936) · [Code](https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/ExpeL)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Voyager](#/papers/wang2023voyager "Voyager: An Open-Ended Embodied Agent with Large Language Models (2024)")**: An Open-Ended Embodied Agent with Large Language Models (TMLR 2024). A GPT-4 agent in Minecraft that writes its actions as code and keeps the programs that work in an ever-growing skill library, indexed by the embedding of their description and retrieved for new tasks; an automatic curriculum proposes the next task (abstract; §2.1–2.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2023voyager) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.16291) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.16291) · [Code](https://github.com/MineDojo/Voyager)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>

<a id="prompt-and-program-optimization"></a>

### Prompt and program optimization


<a id="context-skills-and-harness-code"></a>

#### Context, skills and harness code

- **[ACE](#/papers/zhang2025ace "Agentic Context Engineering: Evolving Contexts for Self-Improving Language Models (2026)")**: Agentic Context Engineering: Evolving Contexts for Self-Improving Language Models (ICLR 2026). Contexts as evolving playbooks, built by a generator, reflector and curator. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhang2025ace) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.04618) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04618) · [Code](https://github.com/ace-agent/ace)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Combee](#/papers/li2026combee "Combee: Scaling Parallel Prompt Learning for Self-Improving LLM Agents (2026)")**: Scaling Parallel Prompt Learning for Self-Improving LLM Agents (COLM 2026). Runs ACE/GEPA-style prompt learning in parallel over many agent traces. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2026combee) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.04247) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04247)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[SkillOpt](#/papers/yang2026skillopt "SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills (2026)")**: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills (preprint 2026). Trains an agent's skill document like weights: bounded add/delete/replace edits accepted only on validation gains. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/yang2026skillopt) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.23904) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23904)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Meta-Harness](#/papers/lee2026metaharness "Meta-Harness: End-to-End Optimization of Model Harnesses (2026)")**: End-to-End Optimization of Model Harnesses (COLM 2026 style). - **[Coding Agents are Strong Prompt Optimizers](#/papers/singh2026casd "Coding Agents are Strong Prompt Optimizers (2026)")** (preprint 2026). A coding agent analyzes a static corpus of trajectories with its own code and writes behavioral rules into the prompt. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/singh2026casd) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.26261) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26261)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[DIVE](#/papers/xiong2026dive "DIVE: Unlocking Self-Improvement in Frozen Language Models Through Diversity-Driven Skill Evolution (2026)")**: Unlocking Self-Improvement in Frozen Language Models Through Diversity-Driven Skill Evolution (preprint 2026). Evolves several populations of natural-language skills from experience and verifier feedback. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/xiong2026dive) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.12486) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12486)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[RuleEvolve](#/papers/jiang2026ruleevolve "Self-Evolving Coding Rules for AI Coding Agents (2026)")**: Self-Evolving Coding Rules for AI Coding Agents (NeurIPS 2026). Evolves a pool of coding-agent rules: an LLM mutates candidates and a judge keeps the best (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/jiang2026ruleevolve) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.00650) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00650)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[ADAS](#/papers/hu2024adas "Automated Design of Agentic Systems (2024)")**: Automated Design of Agentic Systems (ICLR 2025 (per the paper's running header, PDF p. 1), 2024). Meta Agent Search: a meta agent programs new agents in code (abstract), building on an archive of discovered agents (App. B). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/hu2024adas) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2408.08435) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08435) · [Code](https://github.com/ShengranHu/ADAS)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>

Searches the workflow itself: code-represented graphs of LLM-calling nodes (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhang2024aflow) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2410.10762) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10762) · [Code](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/AFlow)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>

Self-play that optimizes the inference-time context of agents in multi-turn, multi-agent text games (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/xie2026memo) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.09022) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09022) · [Code](https://github.com/openverse-ai/MEMO)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[GRACE](#/papers/hsu2026grace "Scoped Verification for Reliable Long-Horizon Agentic Context Evolution under Distribution Shift (2026)")**: Scoped Verification for Reliable Long-Horizon Agentic Context Evolution under Distribution Shift (preprint 2026). Keeps an agent's persistent system instruction as a typed semantic graph, updated from operational experience while the model, tools and harness stay fixed (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/hsu2026grace) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.09175) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09175) · [Code](https://github.com/RedMind-Research/GRACE)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[HarnessLens ("Verify Smarter](#/papers/xu2026harnesslens "Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification (2026)")**: Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification (Evolve Further"), preprint 2026). Budget-aware evolution of agent harnesses (instructions, tools, runtime components) (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/xu2026harnesslens) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.27311) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311) · [Code](https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[RoboPhD](#/papers/borthwick2026robophd "RoboPhD: Self-Improving Text-to-SQL Through Autonomous Agent Evolution (2026)")**: Self-Improving Text-to-SQL Through Autonomous Agent Evolution (preprint 2026). An evolution agent rewrites a text-to-SQL agent (a database-analysis script and SQL-generation instructions) from performance feedback, with ELO-based selection (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/borthwick2026robophd) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.01126) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01126) · [Code](https://github.com/andborth/RoboPhD)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-sql">harness-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-gen">llm-gen</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-misc">nl2sql-misc</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a></span>
- **[Self-Modifying Lean Proof Agents](#/papers/li2026selfmodifying "Self-Modifying Lean Proof Agents with Verifier-Grounded Benchmark Coevolution (2026)")**: Self-Modifying Lean Proof Agents with Verifier-Grounded Benchmark Coevolution (preprint 2026). A fixed, trusted runtime wraps a mutable workspace (proof workflow, prompts, tools) that the agent rewrites between generations, around one fixed LLM backend (abstract; App. A). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2026selfmodifying) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.17352) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17352)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-general">itp-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Large Language Models as Tool Makers](#/papers/cai2023toolmakers "Large Language Models as Tool Makers (2024)")** (ICLR 2024). A strong model writes a reusable Python function (a tool) for a class of tasks; a lighter model solves each instance by calling it (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/cai2023toolmakers) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.17126) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.17126) · [Code](https://github.com/ctlllll/LLM-ToolMaker)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[SKILL-DISCO](#/papers/guo2026skilldisco "SKILL-DISCO: Distilling and Compiling Agent Traces into Reusable Procedural Skills (2026)")**: Distilling and Compiling Agent Traces into Reusable Procedural Skills (preprint 2026). Distills the procedure shared by an agent's successful traces and compiles it into callable, executable skills (abstract): Python procedures, not prompts (§3.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/guo2026skilldisco) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.26669) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26669)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Recursive self-improvement of AI research agents](#/papers/srikanth2026aide2 "Recursive self-improvement of AI research agents (2026)")** (preprint 2026 (Weco AI)). An outer-loop research agent rewrites the code of an inner-loop tree-search agent (a pared-down AIDE), grades each rewrite on ML-engineering, heuristic-algorithm and harness-engineering tasks under a fixed dollar budget, and keeps the best-graded agent as the next one to edit (abstract; §2.1–2.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/srikanth2026aide2) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.26457) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26457)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/compact">compact</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Darwin Gödel Machine (DGM)](#/papers/zhang2025dgm "Darwin G\'odel Machine: Open-Ended Evolution of Self-Improving Agents (2026)")**: Darwin G\"odel Machine: Open-Ended Evolution of Self-Improving Agents (ICLR 2026). A coding agent edits its own code; each new agent is scored on coding benchmarks and archived if it still compiles and can edit code, so the Gödel machine's proof that a change helps is replaced by empirical validation (abstract; §3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhang2025dgm) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2505.22954) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22954) · [Code](https://github.com/jennyzzt/dgm)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[SAGE (Statistical Acceptance Gate)](#/papers/wang2026sage "SAGE: A Statistical Acceptance Gate for Self-Evolving Agents (2026)")**: SAGE: A Statistical Acceptance Gate for Self-Evolving Agents (preprint 2026). An acceptance gate for agents that evolve a skill document: instead of keeping any edit that raises the aggregate validation score, it compares the current and the edited skill item by item, weighs a regression more than a repair, caps the share of solved items an edit may break, and commits only when a one-sided exact binomial test on the discordant items rejects, abstaining otherwise (abstract; §3.2–3.3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2026sage) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.36043) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36043)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/stats">stats</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[GRASP: Gated Regression-Aware Skill Proposer for Self-Improving LLM Agents](#/papers/moll2026grasp "GRASP: Gated Regression-Aware Skill Proposer for Self-Improving LLM Agents (2026)")** (EMNLP 2026). Improves an LLM agent through edits to a bounded library of skills (Markdown instructions injected into its context), with no weight updates: an LLM labels each failed trace with a failure mode, and a skill writer proposes add, modify or remove edits for the most frequent modes (abstract; §2.1–2.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/moll2026grasp) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.29668) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29668) · [Code](https://github.com/jomoll/GRASP)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Certified Long-Horizon Code Agent Evolution via Validation-Gated Skill Optimization](#/papers/wang2026valve "Certified Long-Horizon Code Agent Evolution via Validation-Gated Skill Optimization (2026)")** (preprint 2026). Long-horizon self-evolution of a code agent with frozen weights: after a task with mixed outcomes, the same LLM contrasts successful and failed trajectories to propose skills and principles, which join a persistent bank only if a paired rerun on a fixed 50-task validation panel gains at least 0.02 in pass rate; a retriever that scores embedding similarity, word overlap and an importance prior (App. B.5) picks items for later tasks (§1; §4.1; App. B.6). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2026valve) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.32990) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32990)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[VaG ("When Self-Evolution Backfires")](#/papers/shang2026vag "When Self-Evolution Backfires: Pre-Commit Gating against Skill Contamination in LLM Agents (2026)")**: When Self-Evolution Backfires: Pre-Commit Gating against Skill Contamination in LLM Agents (preprint 2026). Finds that a self-evolving agent that admits every skill it distills from its own trajectories improves, peaks and then degrades as the pool grows, and argues that removing a defective skill afterwards cannot undo the skills later distilled with it in context (abstract; Introduction; Methodology). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/shang2026vag) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.05810) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05810)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[DAEDALUS](#/papers/edy2026daedalus "DAEDALUS: Bootstrapping Agent Memory from Self-Generated Tasks (2026)")**: Bootstrapping Agent Memory from Self-Generated Tasks (preprint 2026). Builds an agent's memory with no training tasks and no oracle verifier: an Explorer proposes tasks with success conditions and tunes their difficulty, a Solver attempts them, and a heuristic written from each failure is kept only after the Solver then succeeds with it several times in a row; the kept heuristics are merged into one bank given to the agent at test time (abstract; §3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/edy2026daedalus) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.08048) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08048) · [Code](https://github.com/illuin-tech/daedalus)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Not All Skills Help: Measuring and Repairing Agent Knowledge](#/papers/wang2026assay "Not All Skills Help: Measuring and Repairing Agent Knowledge (2026)")** (preprint 2026). Curates an agent's natural-language skill library by measurement rather than LLM judgment: randomized masking on a small development set gives each skill a causal score per development task, from which it splits skills whose effect varies across tasks into conditional variants (an LLM rewrites them, behind a development-set gate), retires inert ones, merges near-duplicates, and at inference suppresses the skills predicted to hurt each test task, using its nearest development tasks (abstract; §2.1–2.3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2026assay) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.15390) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15390)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[ADMITOR ("Admission Without Answers")](#/papers/lian2026admitor "Admission Without Answers: Label-Free Certification and Experience Learning for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling (2026)")**: Admission Without Answers: Label-Free Certification and Experience Learning for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling (preprint 2026). A label-free admission gate for an agent that learns skills for optimization modeling (LLM-written LP and MILP models): three model families each write a model, every model is solved on the stated instance and on instances with resampled parameters, the largest group whose optimal values agree on every instance across families gives the accepted value, and a threshold fitted on solver-verified synthetic problems turns the agreement into accept, abstain or escalate, with a Clopper–Pearson bound on false certificates (abstract; §3, Prop. 2). Only the host's own trajectories whose answer equals the accepted value enter its skill library (§3, Step 5). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/lian2026admitor) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.15565) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15565) · [Code](https://github.com/junbolian/AdmitOR)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/labels">labels</a><a class="tag" href="#/tags/stats">stats</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-general">cex-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[Ratchet: How Reliable Must an LLM Judge Be to Retire a Skill?](#/papers/zhang2026ratchet "Ratchet: How Reliable Must an LLM Judge Be to Retire a Skill? (2026)")** (preprint 2026). A skill library kept by one frozen LLM in five roles over an append-only log: a router picks at most one skill per task, a critic labels failures, a synthesizer writes skills from clustered failure patterns, and a curator retires a skill once its measured contribution (passes minus failures over its routed trials) falls to −τ after enough trials and caps the active library at a fixed width (§3); the authors prove a floor on how far below the no-skill control a governed library can fall, finite because of the width and τ (§4, Prop. 1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhang2026ratchet) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.22148) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22148) · [Code](https://github.com/amazon-science/Self-Evolving-Agents-Ratchet)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/judge-general">judge-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Scope Before You Persist](#/papers/cheng2026scope "Scope Before You Persist: Preventing Cross-Family Interference in Agent Memory (2026)")**: Preventing Cross-Family Interference in Agent Memory (preprint 2026). A frozen model edits a 360-character skill policy for a frozen actor round by round, and a gate (ORC: public examples, private tests and metamorphic checks, each with a paired-bootstrap bound, no regression on earlier families) decides each edit; the authors then retrieve each accepted skill only for the task family that produced it (§1; §3; §4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/cheng2026scope) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29144) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29144)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-general">hacking-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-feedback">promptopt-feedback</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[Dynamic Agent Skills: A Lifecycle Survey and Taxonomy of Evolving Skill Libraries](#/papers/li2026dynamicskills "Dynamic Agent Skills: A Lifecycle Survey and Taxonomy of Evolving Skill Libraries (2026)")** (TMLR 2026). Surveys how LLM agents' skill libraries (code functions, natural-language instructions, SKILL.md packages, workflow graphs, learned adapters) change over time, across what it calls a 124-paper 2023–2026 audit set (abstract; §2.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2026dynamicskills) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.10113) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10113)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>
- **[ChipMEM](#/papers/alrabah2026chipmem "ChipMEM: Verification-Grounded Memory for EDA Agents (2026)")**: Verification-Grounded Memory for EDA Agents (preprint 2026). A memory layer for LLM agents that optimize RTL hardware designs or write testbenches: skills retrieved by task similarity, plus Bayesian (Beta) estimates of tool-call success that advise on retries (abstract; §3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/alrabah2026chipmem) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.27067) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27067) · [Code](https://github.com/aalrabah/ChipMEM)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-general">harness-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-promptopt">harness-promptopt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-context">promptopt-context</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a></span>

<a id="foundations-and-the-dspy-line"></a>

#### Foundations and the DSPy line

- **[DSPy](#/papers/khattab2023dspy "DSPy: Compiling Declarative Language Model Calls into Self-Improving Pipelines (2024)")**: Compiling Declarative Language Model Calls into Self-Improving Pipelines (ICLR 2024). Declarative LM pipelines with a compiler that bootstraps and selects few-shot demonstrations (or finetunes) to maximize a metric; instructions are named as parameters, but this version focuses on demonstrations (§4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/khattab2023dspy) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.03714) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.03714) · [Code](https://github.com/stanfordnlp/dspy)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-misc">promptopt-misc</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[GEPA](#/papers/agrawal2025gepa "GEPA: Reflective Prompt Evolution Can Outperform Reinforcement Learning (2026)")**: Reflective Prompt Evolution Can Outperform Reinforcement Learning (ICLR 2026). Reflective prompt evolution with Pareto selection. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/agrawal2025gepa) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2507.19457) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19457) · [Code: gepa](https://github.com/gepa-ai/gepa) · [Code: gepa-ai](https://github.com/gepa-ai/dspy) · [Code: gepa-artifact](https://github.com/gepa-ai/gepa-artifact)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-evolve">promptopt-evolve</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-general">scaling-general</a></span>
- **[MIPRO (shipped in DSPy as MIPROv2)](#/papers/opsahlong2024mipro "Optimizing Instructions and Demonstrations for Multi-Stage Language Model Programs (2024)")**: Optimizing Instructions and Demonstrations for Multi-Stage Language Model Programs (EMNLP 2024). Optimizes instructions and few-shot demonstrations of every module in a multi-stage LM program. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/opsahlong2024mipro) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2406.11695) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11695) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2024.emnlp-main.525)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-general">promptopt-general</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/promptopt-misc">promptopt-misc</a></span>
- **[mmGRPO](#/papers/ziems2025mmgrpo "Composing Policy Gradients and Prompt Optimization for Language Model Programs (2026)")**: Composing Policy Gradients and Prompt Optimization for Language Model Programs (ACM CAIS 2026). - **[FAPO](#/papers/kassianik2026fapo "FAPO: Fully Automated Prompt Optimization of Multi-Step LLM Pipelines (2026)")**: Fully Automated Prompt Optimization of Multi-Step LLM Pipelines (preprint 2026).
