<a id="benchmarks-and-systems-that-consume-equivalence-checking"></a>

### Benchmarks and systems that consume equivalence checking

- **[SQL-RewriteBench](#/papers/long2026sqlrewritebench "SQL-RewriteBench: A Correctness-Gated, Full-Denominator Benchmark for Statement-Level SQL Rewriting (2026)")**: A Correctness-Gated, Full-Denominator Benchmark for Statement-Level SQL Rewriting (preprint Jul 2026). Executable rewrite instances in EQUIV / PERF / ROBUST / PG-aware pools, with *correctness gating*. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/long2026sqlrewritebench) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.09251) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09251) · [Code](https://github.com/SQL-RewriteBench/benchmark)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-gen">llm-gen</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairs-rewrite">pairs-rewrite</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-eval">rewrite-eval</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-llm">rewrite-llm</a></span>
- **[Argus](#/papers/mang2025oracles "Automated Discovery of Test Oracles for Database Management Systems Using LLMs (2025)")**: Automated Discovery of Test Oracles for Database Management Systems Using LLMs (SIGMOD (PACMMOD), 2025). LLMs discover DBMS test oracles as Constrained Abstract Queries (equivalent-query skeletons). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/mang2025oracles) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.06663) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06663) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3802017) · [Code](https://github.com/joyemang33/Argus)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-sql">hacking-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-sql">harness-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nondet-eval">nondet-eval</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-check">pairgen-check</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[DSB](#/papers/ding2021dsb "DSB: a decision support benchmark for workload-driven and traditional database systems (2021)")**: a decision support benchmark for workload-driven and traditional database systems (PVLDB 14(13) 2021). TPC-DS adapted for workload-driven systems: skewed, correlated data and new join-heavy templates. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/ding2021dsb) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3484224.3484234) · [Code](https://github.com/microsoft/dsb)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/workload">workload</a></span>
- **[Qr-Hint](#/papers/hu2024qrhint "Qr-Hint: Actionable Hints Towards Correcting Wrong SQL Queries (2024)")**: Actionable Hints Towards Correcting Wrong SQL Queries (SIGMOD 2024 (PACMMOD)). Given a correct target query and a wrong working query, gives step-by-step repairs (repair sites and fixes), clause by clause from FROM to SELECT, that the authors claim provably lead to a query equivalent to the target (abstract; §1; Theorem 3.1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/hu2024qrhint) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2404.04352) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04352) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3654995) · [Code](https://github.com/yihaoh/qr-hint)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a></span>
- **[SQLRepair](#/papers/preslermarshall2021sqlrepair "SQLRepair: Identifying and Repairing Mistakes in Student-Authored SQL Queries (2021)")**: Identifying and Repairing Mistakes in Student-Authored SQL Queries (ICSE-SEET 2021). Collects the SQL queries that undergraduates wrote for programming-by-example problems, each given as (source, destination) table pairs that a query must turn one into the other, and classifies their syntax and semantic errors (abstract; §II; §IV-A). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/preslermarshall2021sqlrepair) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2102.05729) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2102.05729) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICSE-SEET52601.2021.00030) · [Code](https://github.com/kpresler/SQLRepair)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/smt-misc">smt-misc</a></span>

<a id="database-foundations"></a>

## Database foundations


<a id="rule-based-query-rewriting-non-llm"></a>

### Rule-based query rewriting (non-LLM)

- **[Unnesting Arbitrary Queries](#/papers/neumann2015unnesting "Unnesting Arbitrary Queries (2015)")** (BTW 2015). Claims to unnest arbitrary correlated subqueries ("the dependent join can be eliminated from any query", §3.2) by pushing dependent joins down until they become regular joins. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/neumann2015unnesting) · [Paper](https://dl.gi.de/handle/20.500.12116/2418)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[Optimization of Nested SQL Queries Revisited](#/papers/ganski1987nested "Optimization of Nested SQL Queries Revisited (1987)")** (SIGMOD 1987). Shows that Kim's unnesting algorithm NEST-JA is wrong for COUNT over empty groups (the "COUNT bug", found by Kiessling in a 1984 memo, §5.1) and for join predicates other than equality (§5.3); the outer-join fix for the first then fails when the outer relation has duplicates in the join column (§5.4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/ganski1987nested) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/38713.38723) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/38713.38723)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[WeTune](#/papers/wang2022wetune "WeTune: Automatic Discovery and Verification of Query Rewrite Rules (2022)")**: Automatic Discovery and Verification of Query Rewrite Rules (SIGMOD 2022). Discovers rewrite rules by enumerating small plan-template pairs and their enabling constraints. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2022wetune) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/3514221.3526125) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3514221.3526125) · [Code](https://github.com/WeTune/WeTune-code)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-apply">pairgen-apply</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-smt">rewrite-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[QueryBooster](#/papers/bai2023querybooster "QueryBooster: Improving SQL Performance Using Middleware Services for Human-Centered Query Rewriting (2023)")**: Improving SQL Performance Using Middleware Services for Human-Centered Query Rewriting (PVLDB 16(11) 2023). Human-centered rewriting middleware: users write rules (VarSQL) or give example pairs it generalizes. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/bai2023querybooster) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.08272) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.08272) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3611479.3611497) · [Code](https://github.com/ISG-ICS/QueryBooster)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[SqlRewriter](#/papers/bai2026sqlrewriter "SqlRewriter: Harnessing Community Knowledge to Rewrite SQL Queries (2026)")**: Harnessing Community Knowledge to Rewrite SQL Queries (ICDE 2026 (demo)). A community platform for contributing and sharing rewrite rules. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/bai2026sqlrewriter) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde65706.2026.00326)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[LearnedRewrite](#/papers/zhou2021learnedrewrite "A learned query rewrite system using Monte Carlo tree search (2021)")**: A learned query rewrite system using Monte Carlo tree search (PVLDB 15(1) 2022, 2021). Chooses the *order* of Calcite rewrite rules by Monte Carlo tree search. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhou2021learnedrewrite) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3485450.3485456) · [Code](https://github.com/XuanheZhou/LearnedRewrite)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-apply">pairgen-apply</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-learned">qo-learned</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a></span>
- **[SlabCity](#/papers/dong2023slabcity "SlabCity: Whole-Query Optimization Using Program Synthesis (2023)")**: Whole-Query Optimization Using Program Synthesis (PVLDB 16(11) 2023). Rule-free whole-query rewriting by program synthesis, with a new LeetCode benchmark with constraints. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/dong2023slabcity) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3611479.3611515) · [Code](https://github.com/eidos06/SlabCity)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/hacking-sql">hacking-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-eval">rewrite-eval</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-smt">rewrite-smt</a></span>
- **[GRewriter](#/papers/jiang2025grewriter "GRewriter: Practical Query Rewriting with Automatic Rule Set Expansion in GaussDB (2025)")**: Practical Query Rewriting with Automatic Rule Set Expansion in GaussDB (PVLDB 18(12) 2025). Huawei GaussDB's rewriter: rules in G-DSL found by a WeTune-derived enumerator. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/jiang2025grewriter) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3750601.3750622)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-apply">pairgen-apply</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[SLER](#/papers/zhang2026sler "Efficient Query Rewrite Rule Discovery via Standardized Enumeration and Learning-to-Rank (2026)")**: Efficient Query Rewrite Rule Discovery via Standardized Enumeration and Learning-to-Rank (preprint 2026). Rule discovery with standardized plan templates and a learning-to-rank pre-filter, scaling to larger plans. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhang2026sler) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.04169) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04169)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-apply">pairgen-apply</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-smt">rewrite-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[ARG](#/papers/li2025arg "ARG: Testing Query Rewriters via Abstract Rule Guided Fuzzing (2025)")**: Testing Query Rewriters via Abstract Rule Guided Fuzzing (ASE 2025). Fuzzes query rewriters with queries steered toward untested "abstract rules". <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2025arg) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00151)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-eval">rewrite-eval</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[Starburst query rewrite](#/papers/pirahesh1992starburst "Extensible/Rule Based Query Rewrite Optimization in Starburst (1992)")**: Extensible/Rule Based Query Rewrite Optimization in Starburst (SIGMOD 1992). An extensible rule engine that rewrites queries in the Query Graph Model (QGM) before plan optimization. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/pirahesh1992starburst) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/130283.130294) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/130283.130294)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>

<a id="query-optimizers-and-their-frameworks"></a>

### Query optimizers and their frameworks

- **[The Volcano Optimizer Generator](#/papers/graefe1993volcano "The Volcano optimizer generator: extensibility and efficient search (1993)")**: The Volcano optimizer generator: extensibility and efficient search (ICDE 1993). An optimizer generator: transformation and implementation rules in, a memoizing top-down search out. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/graefe1993volcano) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde.1993.344061)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[The Cascades Framework for Query Optimization](#/papers/graefe1995cascades "The Cascades Framework for Query Optimization (1995)")** (IEEE Data Eng. Bull. 18(3) 1995). Volcano's successor: rules and tasks as objects, a memo of groups, interleaved exploration and implementation. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/graefe1995cascades) · [Paper](http://sites.computer.org/debull/95SEP-CD.pdf)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[Apache Calcite](#/papers/begoli2018calcite "Apache Calcite: A Foundational Framework for Optimized Query Processing Over Heterogeneous Data Sources (2018)")**: A Foundational Framework for Optimized Query Processing Over Heterogeneous Data Sources (SIGMOD 2018). A framework with parsing, relational algebra, many optimization rules and a Volcano-style planner. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/begoli2018calcite) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.10233) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.10233) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3183713.3190662) · [Code](https://github.com/apache/calcite)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-apply">pairgen-apply</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a></span>
- **[Balsa](#/papers/yang2022balsa "Balsa: Learning a Query Optimizer Without Expert Demonstrations (2022)")**: Learning a Query Optimizer Without Expert Demonstrations (SIGMOD 2022). A learned query optimizer trained by RL without expert demonstrations. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/yang2022balsa) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2201.01441) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2201.01441) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3514221.3517885) · [Code](https://github.com/balsa-project/balsa)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-learned">qo-learned</a></span>

<a id="test-database-generation-and-counterexample-search-non-proving"></a>

### Test-database generation and counterexample search (non-proving)

- **[XData](#/papers/chandra2014xdata "Data generation for testing and grading SQL queries (2015)")**: Data generation for testing and grading SQL queries (VLDB J. 24(6) 2015). Generates small datasets that "kill" query mutants (join-type, comparison, aggregation, …). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chandra2014xdata) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1411.6704) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1411.6704) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00778-015-0395-0)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a></span>
- **[Test Data Generation for Complex SQL Queries](#/papers/somwase2024complex "Test Data Generation for Complex SQL Queries (2025)")** (PACMMOD 3(6) 2025 (SIGMOD 2026)). XData's successor for multi-block, multi-level nested queries (§6; benchmark, §9). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/somwase2024complex) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2409.18821) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18821) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3769832)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a></span>
- **[RATest](#/papers/miao2019ratest "Explaining Wrong Queries Using Small Examples (2019)")**: Explaining Wrong Queries Using Small Examples (SIGMOD 2019). Given a separating database, looks for the smallest sub-instance that still separates a wrong query from the right one (abstract); its faster algorithm, used for the rest of the experiments (§7.1), minimizes the witness of one differing tuple (§4.2, Alg. 2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/miao2019ratest) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1904.04467) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.04467) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3299869.3319866)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/reduce">reduce</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a></span>
- **[Semantic Evaluation for Text-to-SQL with Distilled Test Suites](#/papers/zhong2020testsuite "Semantic Evaluation for Text-to-SQL with Distilled Test Suites (2020)")** (EMNLP 2020). "Test-suite accuracy": a small suite of databases distilled to separate the gold query from near-miss neighbours. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhong2020testsuite) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2010.02840) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2010.02840) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2020.emnlp-main.29) · [Code](https://github.com/taoyds/test-suite-sql-eval)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-eval">nl2sql-eval</a></span>
- **[SQLDriller](#/papers/yang2025sqldriller "Automated Validating and Fixing of Text-to-SQL Translation with Execution Consistency (2025)")**: Automated Validating and Fixing of Text-to-SQL Translation with Execution Consistency (PACMMOD / SIGMOD 2025). Finds and fixes wrong NL→SQL mappings in training data via counterexample databases and "execution consistency". <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/yang2025sqldriller) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3725271)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-gen">llm-gen</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-data">nl2sql-data</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-select">nl2sql-select</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-sql">scaling-sql</a></span>
- **[EvoSQL](#/papers/castelein2018evosql "Search-Based Test Data Generation for SQL Queries (2018)")**: Search-Based Test Data Generation for SQL Queries (ICSE 2018). Generates test data covering each SQLFpc coverage target of a query. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/castelein2018evosql) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3180155.3180202) · [Code: evosql](https://github.com/SERG-Delft/evosql) · [Code: evosql-appendix](https://zenodo.org/records/1166023)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a></span>
- **[ParSEval](#/papers/chen2025parseval "ParSEval: Plan-aware Test Database Generation for SQL Equivalence Evaluation (2025)")**: Plan-aware Test Database Generation for SQL Equivalence Evaluation (PVLDB 18(11) 2025). Generates test databases that aim to cover every branch of the queries' logical plans (abstract; a greedy heuristic, §4.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chen2025parseval) · [PDF](https://dbgroup.cs.tsinghua.edu.cn/jnwang/papers/vldb2025-parseval.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3749646.3749727) · [Code](https://github.com/sfu-db/ParSEval)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-eval">nl2sql-eval</a></span>
- **[data-aware NL2SQL candidate selection](#/papers/kikot2026separating "Data-aware candidate selection in NL2SQL translation via small separating instances (2026)")**: Data-aware candidate selection in NL2SQL translation via small separating instances (preprint 2026). Picks among text-to-SQL candidates using small separating database instances. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/kikot2026separating) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.12319) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12319) · [Code](https://github.com/staskikotx/SISelection)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag" href="#/tags/reduce">reduce</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-search">cex-search</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-data">nl2sql-data</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-select">nl2sql-select</a></span>
- **[DPC (Dual-Paradigm Consistency)](#/papers/li2026dpc "DPC: Training-Free Text-to-SQL Candidate Selection via Dual-Paradigm Consistency (2026)")**: DPC: Training-Free Text-to-SQL Candidate Selection via Dual-Paradigm Consistency (ACL 2026 per the arXiv comment). Picks among text-to-SQL candidates without a gold query: LLM agents build a "Minimal Distinguishing Database" on which the two leading candidates return different results (abstract, §3.3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/li2026dpc) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.15163) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15163) · [Code](https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DPC)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-sql">harness-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-select">nl2sql-select</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/scaling-sql">scaling-sql</a></span>
- **[SynSQL](#/papers/habibollah2026synsql "SynSQL: Synthesizing Relational Databases for Robust Evaluation of Text-to-SQL Systems (2026)")**: Synthesizing Relational Databases for Robust Evaluation of Text-to-SQL Systems (preprint, 2026). LLM-synthesized test databases conditioned on the NL question and schema, not the gold SQL. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/habibollah2026synsql) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.27261) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27261)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-eval">nl2sql-eval</a></span>
- **[Edit Based Grading of SQL Queries](#/papers/chandra2019grading "Edit Based Grading of SQL Queries (2019)")** (arXiv 2019, published at CODS-COMAD 2021; a short version is ICDE 2019 ("Automated Grading of SQL Queries")). Grades student SQL: XData's generated test databases decide correctness, and partial marks come from an edit distance to the nearest correct query. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chandra2019grading) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1912.09019) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1912.09019)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-misc">cex-misc</a></span>

<a id="formal-proving--refuting-of-sql-equivalence"></a>

### Formal proving / refuting of SQL equivalence

- **[VeriEQL](#/papers/he2024verieql "VeriEQL: Bounded Equivalence Verification for Complex SQL Queries with Integrity Constraints (2024)")**: Bounded Equivalence Verification for Complex SQL Queries with Integrity Constraints (OOPSLA 2024). Bounded equivalence verification of complex SQL with integrity constraints. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/he2024verieql) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2403.03193) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03193) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3649849) · [Code: VeriEQL](https://github.com/VeriEQL/VeriEQL) · [Code: VeriEQL-artifact-evaluation](https://zenodo.org/records/10795614)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-smt">bounded-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairs-check">pairs-check</a></span>
- **[Polygon](#/papers/zhao2025polygon "Polygon: Symbolic Reasoning for SQL using Conflict-Driven Under-Approximation Search (2025)")**: Symbolic Reasoning for SQL using Conflict-Driven Under-Approximation Search (PLDI 2025). Generates an input database on which several queries' outputs satisfy a property, e.g. two queries differ. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhao2025polygon) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2504.06542) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06542) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3729303) · [Code: polygon-artifact-evaluation](https://zenodo.org/records/15059866) · [Code: polygon-sql](https://github.com/polygon-sql/polygon)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-smt">bounded-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a></span>
- **[SpotIt](#/papers/klopfenstein2025spotit "SpotIt: Evaluating Text-to-SQL Evaluation with Formal Verification (2026)")**: Evaluating Text-to-SQL Evaluation with Formal Verification (ICLR 2026). Evaluates text-to-SQL by searching, with bounded verification, for a database that separates generated and gold SQL. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/klopfenstein2025spotit) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.26840) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26840) · [Code](https://github.com/ai-ar-research/SpotIt-plus)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-smt">bounded-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-gen">llm-gen</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-eval">nl2sql-eval</a></span>
- **[SpotIt+](#/papers/tremante2026spotitplus "SpotIt+: Verification-based Text-to-SQL Evaluation with Database Constraints (2026)")**: Verification-based Text-to-SQL Evaluation with Database Constraints (preprint, 2026). SpotIt plus a best-effort constraint-mining pipeline, so counterexample databases are more realistic (abstract; §1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/tremante2026spotitplus) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.04334) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04334) · [Code](https://github.com/ai-ar-research/SpotIt-plus)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-smt">bounded-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nl2sql-eval">nl2sql-eval</a></span>
- **[SQLSolver](#/papers/ding2023sqlsolver "Proving Query Equivalence Using Linear Integer Arithmetic (2023)")**: Proving Query Equivalence Using Linear Integer Arithmetic (PACMMOD 1(4) / SIGMOD 2024, 2023). Unbounded prover that handles U-expression *unbounded summations* via LIA\* (linear integer arithmetic with stars). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/ding2023sqlsolver) · [PDF](https://www.cs.yale.edu/homes/piskac/papers/2024SIGMOD.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626768) · [Code](https://github.com/SJTU-IPADS/SQLSolver)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-discover">rules-discover</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[QED](#/papers/wang2024qed "QED: A Powerful Query Equivalence Decider for SQL (2024)")**: A Powerful Query Equivalence Decider for SQL (PVLDB 17(11) 2024). Q-expressions (semiring semantics), with SMT used in normalization and unification; NULLs, keys and foreign keys modelled. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2024qed) · [PDF](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol17/p3602-wang.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3681954.3682024)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a></span>
- **[Verifying SQL Queries using Theories of Tables and Relations](#/papers/mohamed2024cvc5sql "Verifying SQL Queries using Theories of Tables and Relations (2024)")** (LPAR 2024). Extends cvc5's SMT theories of bags (as a theory of tables) and of relations, and adds a theory of nullable sorts, for SQL queries with join, projection and selection (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/mohamed2024cvc5sql) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2405.03057) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03057) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.29007/rlt7) · [Code](https://github.com/cvc5/cvc5)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a></span>
- **[Logos](#/papers/ke2026logos "Logos: Certified Order-Sensitive SQL Rewrites with Mechanized Semantics and LLM Guidance (2026)")**: Certified Order-Sensitive SQL Rewrites with Mechanized Semantics and LLM Guidance (preprint Aug 2026). Order-sensitive SQL semantics mechanized in Rocq (FormalSQL); an LLM proof agent proves equivalence or proposes a counter-database. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/ke2026logos) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.15709) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15709) · [Code: FormalSQL](https://github.com/WindOctober/FormalSQL) · [Code: Logos](https://github.com/WindOctober/Logos)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-llm">cex-llm</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/harness-sql">harness-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-sql">itp-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-assist">llm-assist</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/llm-method">llm-method</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nondet-semantics">nondet-semantics</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-itp">prove-itp</a></span>
- **[HoTTSQL](#/papers/chu2016hottsql "HoTTSQL: proving query rewrites with univalent SQL semantics (2017)")**: proving query rewrites with univalent SQL semantics (PLDI 2017). Machine-checkable denotational SQL semantics in Coq: K-relations plus homotopy type theory, covering bags, correlated subqueries, aggregation. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chu2016hottsql) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1607.04822) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1607.04822) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3062341.3062348) · [Code](https://github.com/uwdb/Cosette)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-sql">itp-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-itp">prove-itp</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[Cosette](#/papers/chu2017cosette "Cosette: An Automated Prover for SQL (2017)")**: An Automated Prover for SQL (CIDR 2017). Automated SQL prover: a Rosette/SMT bounded search for counterexamples plus a Coq prover for equivalence. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chu2017cosette) · [Paper](https://www.cidrdb.org/cidr2017/papers/p51-chu-cidr17.pdf) · [Code](https://github.com/uwdb/Cosette)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-smt">bounded-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-sql">itp-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-itp">prove-itp</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[Speeding up symbolic reasoning for relational queries](#/papers/wang2018symbolic "Speeding up symbolic reasoning for relational queries (2018)")** (OOPSLA 2018). Refines a bounded check's symbolic search space by backward symbolic provenance analysis before SMT solving. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2018symbolic) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3276527)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-smt">bounded-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/cex-smt">cex-smt</a></span>
- **[UDP](#/papers/chu2018udp "Axiomatic foundations and algorithms for deciding semantic equivalences of SQL queries (2018)")**: Axiomatic foundations and algorithms for deciding semantic equivalences of SQL queries (PVLDB 11(11) 2018). U-semirings: semiring semantics with unbounded summation and duplicate elimination, implemented in a proof assistant. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chu2018udp) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.02229) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.02229) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3236187.3236200) · [Code](https://github.com/uwdb/Cosette)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-sql">itp-sql</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-itp">prove-itp</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[EQUITAS](#/papers/zhou2019equitas "Automated verification of query equivalence using satisfiability modulo theories (2019)")**: Automated verification of query equivalence using satisfiability modulo theories (PVLDB 12(11) 2019). SMT-based prover: a query becomes a symbolic representation (SR) ⟨COND, COLS, ASSIGN⟩ over symbolic tuples, and containment is checked with Z3. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhou2019equitas) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3342263.3342267)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a></span>
- **[SPES](#/papers/zhou2020spes "SPES: A Symbolic Approach to Proving Query Equivalence Under Bag Semantics (2022)")**: A Symbolic Approach to Proving Query Equivalence Under Bag Semantics (ICDE 2022). Proves equivalence under **bag** semantics symbolically. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/zhou2020spes) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2004.00481) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2004.00481) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde53745.2022.00250) · [Code](https://github.com/georgia-tech-db/spes)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a></span>
- **[Query Weak Equivalence and its Verification in Analytical Databases](#/papers/you2025weakeq "Query Weak Equivalence and its Verification in Analytical Databases (2025)")** (ICDE 2025). "Weak equivalence": non-equivalent queries that agree on the *current* database; a Query Lattice caches OLAP results. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/you2025weakeq) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde65448.2025.00144)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/smt-misc">smt-misc</a></span>
- **[RuleScript](#/papers/pan2026rulescript "An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules (2026)")**: An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules (preprint May 2026). An engine-independent DSL for query rewrite rules, with matching and transformation phases. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/pan2026rulescript) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05536) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/pairgen-apply">pairgen-apply</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-smt">rewrite-smt</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a></span>
- **[Mediator](#/papers/wang2017mediator "Verifying Equivalence of Database-Driven Applications (2017)")**: Verifying Equivalence of Database-Driven Applications (PACMPL 2(POPL) 2018 (POPL 2018), 2017). Verifies equivalence (and refinement) of two database-driven programs over different schemas, as in schema refactoring (abstract; refinement, §1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/wang2017mediator) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1710.07660) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1710.07660) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3158144)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/prove-smt">prove-smt</a></span>

<a id="formal-sql-semantics"></a>

### Formal SQL semantics

- **[A Formal Semantics of SQL Queries](#/papers/guagliardo2017semantics "A formal semantics of SQL queries, its validation, and applications (2017)")**: A formal semantics of SQL queries, its validation, and applications (Its Validation, and Applications, PVLDB 11(1) 2017). Formal semantics of basic SQL with subqueries, bags, set operations and NULLs (3-valued logic). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/guagliardo2017semantics) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3151113.3151116)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-null">theory-null</a></span>
- **[A Coq mechanised formal semantics for realistic SQL queries: formally reconciling SQL and bag relational algebra](#/papers/benzaken2019coq "A Coq mechanised formal semantics for realistic SQL queries: formally reconciling SQL and bag relational algebra (2019)")** (CPP 2019). Executable Coq semantics for SELECT … GROUP BY … HAVING with NULLs, aggregates and correlated subqueries. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/benzaken2019coq) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3293880.3294107)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-sql">itp-sql</a></span>
- **[A Foundation for Conventional and Temporal Query Optimization Addressing Duplicates and Ordering](#/papers/slivinskas2001foundation "A Foundation for Conventional and Temporal Query Optimization Addressing Duplicates and Ordering (2001)")** (IEEE TKDE 13(1) 2001). An algebra over lists, so it keeps duplicates and order, with temporal operations, and six kinds of equivalence: list, multiset and set, plus their snapshot forms (§3, §4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/slivinskas2001foundation) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/69.908979)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nondet-semantics">nondet-semantics</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Formal semantics of SQL queries](#/papers/negri1991semantics "Formal semantics of SQL queries (1991)")** (TODS 16(3) 1991). Defines SQL query semantics by translation into an Extended Three-Valued tuple Predicate Calculus (§1–§3). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/negri1991semantics) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/111197.111212) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/111197.111212)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-null">theory-null</a></span>
- **[A logic for rule-based query optimization in graph-based data models](#/papers/coburn1993logic "A logic for rule-based query optimization in graph-based data models (1993)")** (DOOD 1993 (LNCS 760)). A wide-spectrum algebra and refinement calculus for graph-based data models (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/coburn1993logic) · [PDF](https://link.springer.com/content/pdf/10.1007/3-540-57530-8_8.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1007/3-540-57530-8_8)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/nondet-semantics">nondet-semantics</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/qo-rules">qo-rules</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rewrite-classic">rewrite-classic</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-lib">rules-lib</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/rules-verify">rules-verify</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[A Formalization of SQL with Nulls](#/papers/ricciotti2020nulls "A Formalization of SQL with Nulls (2022)")** (J. Autom. Reasoning 66(4) 2022). A Coq mechanization of SQL semantics with set and bag operations, lateral joins, nested subqueries and NULLs, with key metatheoretic properties validated (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/ricciotti2020nulls) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2003.11331) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2003.11331) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10817-022-09632-4) · [Code](https://github.com/wricciot/nullSQL)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/itp-sql">itp-sql</a></span>

<a id="theory-equivalence-characterizations-bounds-and-test-database-families"></a>

### Theory: equivalence characterizations, bounds and test-database families

- **[Optimal Implementation of Conjunctive Queries in Relational Data Bases](#/papers/chandra1977conjunctive "Optimal Implementation of Conjunctive Queries in Relational Data Bases (1977)")** (STOC 1977). Defines conjunctive queries; equivalence iff homomorphisms both ways between their "natural models" (Lemma 13; later called canonical databases). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chandra1977conjunctive) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/800105.803397)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
- **[Equivalences among Relational Expressions](#/papers/aho1979equivalences "Equivalences among Relational Expressions (1979)")** (SIAM J. Comput. 8(2) 1979). Tableaux for select-project-join expressions; equivalence and minimization via containment mappings. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/aho1979equivalences) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1137/0208017)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
- **[Equivalences Among Relational Expressions with the Union and Difference Operators](#/papers/sagiv1980union "Equivalences Among Relational Expressions with the Union and Difference Operators (1980)")** (J. ACM 27(4) 1980). Unions of tableaux with the same target relation scheme: a union is contained in another iff each member is contained in some member of the other (Theorem 3, PDF p. 9). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/sagiv1980union) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/322217.322221)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
- **[On conjunctive queries containing inequalities](#/papers/klug1988inequalities "On conjunctive queries containing inequalities (1988)")** (J. ACM 35(1) 1988). CQ containment with <, ≤, = over dense orders needs a finite family of representative databases, one per ordering. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/klug1988inequalities) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/42267.42273)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
- **[Optimization of real conjunctive queries](#/papers/chaudhuri1993real "Optimization of real conjunctive queries (1993)")** (PODS 1993). Bag semantics for CQs (SQL without DISTINCT), which it says Dayal et al. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/chaudhuri1993real) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/153850.153856)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Containment of conjunctive queries: beyond relations as sets](#/papers/ioannidis1995bag "Containment of conjunctive queries: beyond relations as sets (1995)")** (TODS 20(3) 1995). Containment under "label systems" (sets, bags and more), with homomorphism-type characterizations. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/ioannidis1995bag) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/211414.211419)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[The containment problem for &lt;bi&gt;Real&lt;/bi&gt; conjunctive queries with inequalities](#/papers/jayram2006inequalities "The containment problem for &lt;bi&gt;Real&lt;/bi&gt; conjunctive queries with inequalities (2006)")** (PODS 2006). Bag containment of CQs with ≠ is undecidable, by reduction from Hilbert's tenth problem. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/jayram2006inequalities) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1142351.1142363)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Bag Semantics Conjunctive Query Containment. Four Small Steps Towards Undecidability.](#/papers/marcinkowski2025smallsteps "Bag Semantics Conjunctive Query Containment. Four Small Steps Towards Undecidability. (2024)")** (PACMMOD 2(2) (PODS 2024)). Undecidability for small generalizations of bag CQ containment. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/marcinkowski2025smallsteps) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.18003) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18003) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3651604)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Bag Semantics Query Containment: The CQ vs. UCQ Case and Other Stories](#/papers/marcinkowski2025cqucq "Bag Semantics Query Containment: The CQ vs. UCQ Case and Other Stories (2025)")** (PACMMOD 2025). Turns a UCQ into a CQ so containment questions transfer. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/marcinkowski2025cqucq) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.07219) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07219) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3767711)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Attacking Diophantus: Special Cases of Bag Containment](#/papers/konstantinidis2026diophantus "Attacking Diophantus: Special Cases of Bag Containment (2026)")** (preprint Sep 2026). A unified framework that decides bag-set containment of join-uniform CQs into arbitrary CQs, subsuming the projection-free and join-on-free cases; bag-bag stays open for join-uniform queries (§1, PDF p. 3; Thm. 6.2). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/konstantinidis2026diophantus) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30956) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30956)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Deciding Equivalences among Conjunctive Aggregate Queries](#/papers/cohen2007aggregate "Deciding Equivalences among Conjunctive Aggregate Queries (2007)")** (J. ACM 54(2) 2007). Equivalence characterizations for CQs with comparisons plus count, min/max or sum (no HAVING); for count-distinct a sufficient condition, also shown necessary in special cases (abstract; §5.1; Thm. 7.3, Cor. 7.4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/cohen2007aggregate) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1219092.1219093)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Equivalence of Queries Combining Set and Bag-Set Semantics](#/papers/cohen2006setbag "Equivalence of Queries Combining Set and Bag-Set Semantics (2006)")** (PODS 2006). "Combined semantics" for SQL mixing DISTINCT parts (set) with plain parts (bag-set). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/cohen2006setbag) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/1142351.1142362) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1142351.1142362)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Equivalence of Queries That Are Sensitive to Multiplicities](#/papers/cohen2009multiplicities "Equivalence of Queries That Are Sensitive to Multiplicities (2009)")** (VLDB J. 18(3) 2009). Extends [Equivalence of Queries Combining…](#/papers/cohen2006setbag "Equivalence of Queries Combining Set and Bag-Set Semantics (2006)") with bag semantics, full proofs and positive multiset queries. <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/cohen2009multiplicities) · [PDF](https://link.springer.com/content/pdf/10.1007/s00778-008-0122-1.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00778-008-0122-1)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[Provenance Semirings](#/papers/green2007provenance "Provenance Semirings (2007)")** (PODS 2007). Relations annotated with elements of a commutative semiring (K-relations): sets, bags, lineage and why-provenance are special cases (§2–4). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/green2007provenance) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/1265530.1265535) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1265530.1265535)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
- **[Few Rows Tell Them Apart](#/papers/cohen2026fewrows "Few Rows Tell Them Apart: Equivalence of Queries Mixing Set and Bag Semantics (2026)")**: Equivalence of Queries Mixing Set and Bag Semantics (preprint Sep 2026). Computable bounds B such that agreement on all databases with ≤ B tuples per relation implies equivalence, for conjunctive queries mixing set and bag-set semantics (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/cohen2026fewrows) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.09978) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09978)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-bag">theory-bag</a></span>
- **[How Can We Shrink the Family of Test Databases? Query Containment with Nulls and Comparisons](#/papers/sternbach2026shrink "How Can We Shrink the Family of Test Databases? Query Containment with Nulls and Comparisons (2026)")** (preprint Sep 2026). Containment of CQs over databases with NULLs (SQL 3-valued logic) and of CQs with order comparisons; both Π₂ᵖ-complete by prior work (abstract). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/sternbach2026shrink) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.16218) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16218)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-null">theory-null</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
- **[querying indefinite order data](#/papers/vandermeyden1992indefinite "The complexity of querying indefinite data about linearly ordered domains (1992)")**: The complexity of querying indefinite data about linearly ordered domains (PODS 1992). Complexity of evaluating positive existential (conjunctive and disjunctive) queries over databases that state only some order facts between points (abstract, §1). <span class="links">[🔎 Summary](#/papers/vandermeyden1992indefinite) · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/137097.137902) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/137097.137902)</span> <span class="tags"><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/bounded-theory">bounded-theory</a><a class="tag sub" href="#/tags/theory-set">theory-set</a></span>
